# Progress

Living status of the project. Read this first; it is updated with every commit. Product decisions are in `PRODUCT.md`, the design system is in `DESIGN.md`, the next steps, research findings and owner direction are in `docs/next-steps.md`, and `git log` has the detail.

## Where we stopped (2026-11-05, the owner's Mac is being wiped; start here)

- **Rules and the reading order for the next person** are in `GUIDELINES.md`. The numbered to-do list, the pre-launch checklist and the owner's direction are in `docs/next-steps.md`. Everything the owner must run or check is in `OPERATIONS.md` (run, deploy, migrations, cron, accounts) and `docs/owner-try-list.md` (things only the owner can see).
- **Latest work:** the Effects tab and the page editor. A creator's header now has three separate settings: the profile picture (their own photo, or one of seven ready-made animated pictures: Peaky Blinders, Apple kitty, Explosive cat, Killua, Angel, Nanami, Naoya), the frame over the photo (none or Black Widow) and the banner effect (rising embers, clutch pulse, falling petals, or one of five animated banner pictures: Sakura, Solo Leveling, Toney, Eternal, Fkimgworkkk). The animated clips are third-party files the owner chose to ship and accepted the licensing risk for (`docs/avatar-decorations-plan.md` section 12). Not yet seen in a browser; see the unticked items in `docs/owner-try-list.md`.
- **Repository clean-up (2026-11-05, the owner's request):** the guideline and setup files were merged into one tool-neutral `GUIDELINES.md` plus `docs/next-steps.md`; `docs/` became `docs/plans/` and `docs/specs/`; `docs/status/` became `docs/status/`; the Windows setup script and local tool folders were dropped from the repo; commit trailers that named a co-author were removed by rewriting the whole history once and force-pushing `main` (the owner's explicit, one-time approval). Anyone with an older clone must delete it and clone again.
- **To resume on a new computer:** clone the repository, run `npm install`, create `.env.local` with the variable names listed in `.env.example` and `OPERATIONS.md` section 6 (the values are in Vercel and the owner's password manager; Vercel's environment page can pull them), then `npm run dev`. Before saying anything is done run `npm run lint`, `npx next typegen && npx tsc --noEmit` and `npm run build`. Browser tests need Chromium (`npx playwright install chromium`); it was not installed on the old Mac, so the Effects page browser specs were written but not run.
- **Known open items:** PayU rejected the business, so paid plans stay closed; migrations 0055 to 0059 and which of them the owner has run are listed in `docs/next-steps.md`. The parked voice auto-learning feature still calls an external model API from `src/lib/tts-learn-run.ts` (off until `TTS_AUTOLEARN` and its API key are set; neither is).

## Earlier state (2026-11-05, avatar decorations Part B: the Dragon's fog banner effect is built; NO migration, nothing to run)

- **Done:** the second half of the Black Widow set, a ready-made banner effect "Dragon's fog" (id `widowfog`): fog and a fir forest roll over the whole header, a dark dragon rises from the right, roars, breathes fire that lights the fog, and sinks back, then it clears; nearly a third of the 14.5 s loop is clean. (First built with a spider and a smoke puff; the owner said the reference had a dragon, so the same day it was redrawn with one.) Drawn by `tools/decor-render/` (`fog.js`, `fog.html`, `render.mjs --scene fog`, README updated) and saved as `public/decorations/widow-fog.webp` (360 by 432, 159 frames, 2.4 MB) plus a 40 KB still. New pieces in the app: `FILE_FX` and `isFileFx` in `src/lib/page-doc/decor.ts`, `wholeHeader` on a custom `DecorRender`, the header block and the Effects tab (preview now covers the whole header), `fxBehind` in `CreatorPanel.tsx` (this effect plays behind the photo and words so it cannot hide the name; first try over the words hid the heading in a real screenshot), a `?fx=widowfog` option in the test fixture. Checked: tsc and eslint pass, production build passes, and the whole browser suite in the Mac's Chrome (desktop and Pixel 5) except `legal-version.spec.ts`, which failed at HEAD for a reason that is not this work (see below); new cases in `test/page-decor.spec.ts` (files present and under 2.6 MB, covers the heading, behind the words, no sideways scroll). Real-header screenshots at 1100 and 390 px wide checked.
- **Found 2026-11-05, fixed the same day:** `test/legal-version.spec.ts` failed (and so CI on GitHub) because the payout wording in the Terms changed after "text 4" was pinned. `TERMS_VERSION` is now "5 November 2026, text 5" with its fingerprint pinned; no paid order existed, so no stored proof is affected. `waitlist-rules` "short @x" cases failed once under load and passed alone.
- **Honest limits:** procedural art; the dragon is a faceted dark silhouette with bright fire, not painted art, and its neck is nearly lost in the fog; the forest is soft dark fir shapes; the transparency is saved with few levels (72 quality) because more doubles the file (the fire light is cut through a wisp texture so no rings show); a faint rectangular band can show in the fog on a frame or two. Not seen on a real phone, with a real photo and login, or watched as a loop (only stills and decoded frames).
- **pfps.gg:** the owner suggested using animated avatars and banners from pfps.gg. Its terms give uploaders ownership and license the files to the site only ("free to use on social media platforms"), nothing about commercial use or re-hosting, and this repo is public. Nothing downloaded; options in section 11 of `docs/avatar-decorations-plan.md`, waiting for the owner.
- **Next:** the owner watches Black Widow and Dragon's fog (`docs/owner-try-list.md`) and decides about pfps.gg; then Flame Fist and Storm Eye, one at a time, 

## Current state (2026-11-05, avatar decorations Part B started: the Black Widow frame is built and drawn by our own code; NO migration)

- **Owner's answers (section 7 of `docs/avatar-decorations-plan.md`):** render each decoration once as a file (not live), the first set of three is right (Black Widow, Flame Fist, Storm Eye), and "move to Part B".
- **Done:** `tools/decor-render/` (README inside) draws the widow in code and records it in the Mac's Chrome: a top-down spider with eight legs solved by a two-bone solver and an alternating-tetrapod stepping gait, body sway and a lagging back, shaded glossy body with a red hourglass, lit legs, cast shadows with height added on the screen (body and knees lifted, shadows on the ground), three little red spiders that crawl out from under its head over the face, and a front leg that lifts and feels the air; one 14 s timeline (rest, turn on the spot, walk head first along the rim, turn, rest on the other side, and back) that closes cleanly (loop gap mean 0.001 of 255; breathing rhythms take a whole number of turns per loop). Output: `public/decorations/black-widow.webp` (480 px, 278 frames, 24 fps while it moves and 15 while it rests, 2.0 MB; encoded as a lossless animation by `img2webp` then re-encoded lossy by `sharp` with its own, lower transparency quality, which is where most bytes were) and `black-widow.poster.webp` (17 KB still of the first frame). It is a new ready-made frame, "Black Widow" (`FILE_FRAMES`, `isFileFrame` in `src/lib/page-doc/decor.ts`), offered in the Effects tab and the page editor and drawn at 220 percent of the photo ring through the existing uploaded-animation path, so the motion rules apply (the file loads only after the page has loaded and only where `mayPlayMotion` allows; otherwise the still). Art size: the canvas is 528 units with the ring circle of radius 120 in its middle (220 percent, the largest frame size); `render.mjs measure` shows nothing is cut off (13 px to spare at the top). Checked: tsc and eslint pass, a production build passes, and in the Mac's Chrome (desktop and Pixel 5) the decor, asset, catalogue, effects-page, page-editor and page-builder specs pass, including new cases for the file frame (files present, size budget 2.6 MB, drawn at 2.2 times the ring, no sideways scroll at 320 px); the test fixture `/dev-fixtures/decor?frame=widow` shows it in the real header. Screenshots of the real header at 1100 and 390 px wide showed the widow and the small spiders on the photo.
- **Not done:** the widow's fog-and-creature banner effect (a whole-header layer, the other half of the reference), Flame Fist and Storm Eye (each its own build); the live-canvas option was not chosen. The look has not been judged by the owner against the Discord recording, not seen on a real phone, and not seen with a real photo and a real login (only a stand-in photo). The motion is checked only as strips of frames (no one has watched the loop play).
- **Next:** the owner watches Black Widow (`docs/owner-try-list.md`) and says what to change; then, one at a time and : the widow's fog banner effect, Flame Fist, Storm Eye. The creator-upload review (moderation, copyright) is still ahead of opening uploads to anyone but the owner.

## Current state (2026-11-05, avatar decorations Part A built: animated WebP uploads, frame size, whole-header effect, paid switch "Photo frame and banner effects"; NO migration beyond 0058)

- **Done, following `docs/avatar-decorations-plan.md` Part A:** (1) an **animated WebP** (up to 6 MB, see-through areas kept, up to 4096 px) is now accepted for the photo frame and banner effect; it is stored as kind `gif` (an animated picture with a still of its first frame, so no migration and the same motion rules as a GIF everywhere); an animated PNG is still refused with a clearer message; still pictures and GIFs stay at 3 MB (`checkAssetFile` in `src/lib/page-doc/assets.ts`). (2) Two new Header settings, parsed with defaults so every stored document still opens: `frameScale` (100 to 220 percent of the photo, 144 to start, slider shown only for a picture frame) and `fxArea` (over the banner only, or over the whole header: banner, photo, name and bio, with no banner strip of its own). (3) A new paid switch `tipDecor`, "Photo frame and banner effects": the Effects tab and the ready-made frames and effects now work for any creator it is ticked for (the page, the save action and the tip page share `mayPickTipDecor` and `wearsTipDecor` in `src/lib/page-doc/decor.ts`); a Custom tip page that is on decides the look itself; it sits in `CUSTOM_KEYS` (ticked by hand, in no plan) and shows on the creator's Paid features page under Your tip page. Creators still do not upload their own files (that stays in the owner's page editor, as the plan said, until moderation and copyright are reviewed). Tests: new cases in `test/page-decor.spec.ts`, `test/page-assets.spec.ts`, `test/plan-catalogue.spec.ts` and a test-only fixture `/dev-fixtures/decor`; tsc and eslint pass, a production build passes, and the page, asset, catalogue, tier and effects tests pass in the Mac's own Chrome. Not seen with a real animated WebP, a real login, or on an iPhone. The nudge up or down for a frame (in the plan) was left out: the art file itself decides where the decoration sits. WebM (transparent) for frames and effects was not added; animated WebP covers it.
- **Next:** Part B (our own decorations: the stepping spider, fog shader, one timeline, rendered once to files; first set Black Widow, Flame Fist, Storm Eye) waits for the owner's answers in section 7 of the plan (the owner has said: owner only for now, paid later, which is now wired). Owner to do: try an animated WebP (`docs/owner-try-list.md`), tick the switch for a test creator.

## Current state (2026-11-04, Paid features page rebuilt; NO migration)

- **Built (an independent review, owner's request):** `/dashboard/plans` no longer says "and N more". The two plan cards are short and equal (name, one line, price, early price, Choose); under them **Every feature, side by side** (`PlanTable.tsx`, server component) names every feature in seven groups with two tick columns, Starter and Creator. Starter's groups are Alert looks, Stream overlays, Stream moments; the Creator-only groups (solid lime bar, "Only in Creator") are Your own media, Your voice, Your fans, Your workflow. A feature on for the creator shows "On for you" and Open; features no plan sells (custom page, meter, pinned messages) show apart as "Switched on for you by hand". The Custom card is replaced by **One-time extras** (`OneTimeExtras.tsx`, list `ONE_TIME_EXTRAS` in `src/lib/pricing.ts`: Custom tip page from ₹2,999, Alert makeover ₹999, QR poster and panels ₹699, Launch kit ₹3,999; placeholders, price shown only while plan prices are shown). The Pressure meter is no longer shown as an item (the key and the code stay). "Your plan" moved above the cards. Big prices use the body font's tabular figures (the mono font put a gap at the comma). Tiers, grants and tests of the tier lists are untouched; `COMPARISON_GROUPS` and `buildComparison` in `src/lib/plan-catalogue.ts` are new and tested.
- **Not built, on purpose:** per-plan quotas (sounds 2/6/20, alert pictures 2/8/30, WebM clips 0/0/10, 3/3/6 MB a picture, 10/40/150 MB in all). The server still has the fixed global limits in `sounds.ts` and `media.ts`, so the page prints no quota numbers until a `planQuota()` enforces them. Whether Free should get 2 alert pictures (it would turn Custom alert media from a Creator feature into a quota) is the owner's decision.
- **Audit (read-only, 3 independent reviewers, 2026-11-04) still open, ranked:** dashboard: nav of up to 12 links whose names do not match the page headings, the overview still says "Payouts have not started yet", a 12-tile overview with stream settings under "Account", alert design split over four pages each with its own preview, Tips and Earnings repeat the same figures, walls of text and jargon, switched-off pages bounce silently to the dashboard, inconsistent page widths and headers. Admin: creator page is one 377-line stack of 9 boxes, Close account sits beside Approve in every list row, the overview repeats the sidebar as 19 tiles and its queue misses media to review, payout requests unpaid 3+ days and unposted tips, stale text ("payouts are not built yet"), page headers and search styled differently per page, eight Money items with jargon. Proposed: a 6-area dashboard (Home, Tips and earnings, Alerts, Tip page, Community, Account) and a 4-job admin (Today, Review content, Money, Setup), tabs on the creator page, a "Needs you" overview. Safe fixes (stale text, Close account moved, labels equal to headings, one off-state page, PageHeader everywhere) need no decision; the regrouping and merges are the owner's call.
- **Infrastructure advice given (not built):** stay on Vercel Hobby and Neon free for now; Vercel Pro (about ₹1,800 a month) once real income starts, because Hobby is non-commercial; Neon free is 100 compute-hours a month and the OBS alert polls every 4 seconds, so five creators could reach it (back off polling when quiet, or Neon Launch pay-as-you-go); move files to Cloudflare R2 (10 GB free, no download fees) when Blob storage passes about 700 MB (needs code changes in the upload paths). Triggers to write into `OPERATIONS.md` when decided.
- **Verified:** `tsc`, `eslint`, the plan catalogue and buy-form tests (24 browser runs and the pure tests) in Chrome on desktop and phone; looked at screenshots at 1440 and 390 px. **Not seen:** the real signed-in page with a real creator.

- **Rules (mine, under the owner's delegation, any can change):** minimum ₹500 available; a creator may request any day (so ₹500 earned today can be asked for tomorrow); UPI tips are available after **1 day** (was 7; `HOLD_DAYS_UPI`), other methods keep 30 days (60 for a creator with no completed payout, so a new creator's card tips wait long: flagged for the owner); one open request at a time; a request is for everything available, at most ₹5,000 for the first payout and ₹25,000 after (constants in `src/lib/payouts.ts`); the owner pays by UPI and records the UTR; a request can be cancelled and the money returns to the creator's balance.
- **Built:** migration `0059_payout_requests.sql` (UPI ID copied onto the request, requested-at, first-payout flag, the owner's name check, cancel reason, one open payout per creator, ledger account `payouts_in_flight`); `src/lib/payouts.ts` (request, mark paid, cancel: each one transaction with the ledger posting and an audit row, requests under a per-creator advisory lock and recomputed from the ledger); the Get paid box and Request button on `/dashboard/earnings` (`RequestPayout.tsx`, `requestMyPayout`); `/admin/payouts` "Payouts to pay" (owner only, Money group, sidebar count and overview queue item): amount, reference FT-n, UPI ID to pay, warning if the creator's UPI ID changed since, days waited, the creator's totals from the ledger (tips, Freetip fee, payment fee, share, paid before), a UTR form with the name tick, Cancel with a reason, and a list of paid and cancelled; an email to the owner on each request (`src/lib/payout-email.ts`, same Resend setup as the contact form, no UPI ID in it). Docs: `OPERATIONS.md` 7k, `docs/payout-design.md` update note, `docs/owner-try-list.md`.
- **Tests:** `test/payouts.spec.ts` (17 runs on the real schema: request, refusals, double request, ceilings, UTR rules, paying once, cancelling with a reversal, totals net of a refund, the email); `test/admin-ops.spec.ts` counts a waiting payout; four older tests moved from 1- and 2-day-old tips to half-day-old ones for the 1-day hold. Type check, lint and the production build pass; the full suite passes apart from the browser-driven specs (Chromium is not installed here, 254 runs) and the known waitlist-rules runs that fail only in a full parallel run.

## Current state (2026-11-04, decisions of the payment-structure talk, saved; docs only)

- **Fee (owner):** 8% in all, kept as a 5% Freetip fee plus a 3% processing charge; no comparing with competitors for now, because many features are free here. Built and pushed (see the entry below).
- **Money flow today:** fans pay exactly the tip through Razorpay into Freetip's one merchant account, which settles to the owner's linked bank account; each creator's share is only a figure on our books until paid, and payouts are blocked on the aggregation question (`docs/rules-check.md`). The bank balance therefore includes creators' money, and Freetip's own share of a ₹100 tip is about ₹5.64 on a domestic card and ₹4.46 on an international card.
- **Razorpay's charges (its pricing pages, read 2026-11-04):** 2% plus 18% GST (2.36%) on domestic cards, UPI, netbanking and wallets; 3% plus GST (3.54%) on international cards, Amex, Diners and EMI; no setup or yearly fee, a one-time ₹199 KYC fee; instant settlement costs extra (from 0.20%); a possible 0.4% UPI fee above ₹2,000 is stated on one page and denied on another (check the dashboard); a new-merchant offer of 0% on domestic payments for 90 days or ₹5 lakh exists for accounts activated from 1 July 2026. NOT confirmed: whether the fee is kept on a refund, whether a lost dispute costs a separate fee, and the owner's own account rates. `/admin/finance/settlements` shows the real provider fees and taxes per settlement; compare them with 2.36%.
- **StreamTipz-style direct routing (fallback, not chosen):** per `docs/competitor-findings.md` creators there receive tips straight into their own UPI or merchant ID (Paytm, PhonePe, Google Pay Business, HDFC SmartHub, BharatPe) and the platform's 5% (7% on a personal UPI route) is deducted from prepaid Alert Tokens (1 token = ₹1). How it confirms a payment is not visible; the notes warn that personal UPI credential or OTP automation is the riskiest pattern; tokens need a way to collect money and may touch stored-value rules (not checked, needs a CA or lawyer). Decision: keep the Razorpay model; consider direct routing, as a second mode, only if Razorpay and Cashfree decline splitting and settling.
- **Done in this session (each has its own entry below):** the 5% + 3% rate, the creator earnings page (`/dashboard/earnings`), and the PayU code pushed with provider-neutral public wording (run migration 0057 first).
- **Waiting on the owner:** the Razorpay and Cashfree replies on splitting payments; run migration 0057; check `/admin/fees` for an older saved pair; the new checks in `docs/owner-try-list.md`.

## Current state (2026-11-04, animated photo frame and banner effect in the custom tip page; NO migration, nothing to run)

The owner asked for Discord-style decoration: an animation over the profile photo and an animated banner, in the AcePressure theme, switchable and not hardcoded, controlled from the admin. Looked up how Discord does it first (Discord's docs: a decoration is an `asset` hash plus a `sku_id` saved on the profile and drawn over the avatar, some are animated PNG; the banner is a separate image or GIF, 680 by 240, and the avatar overlaps its bottom-left corner; Discord's help page mentions no custom uploads, so uploading your own is ours). Built as part of the custom tip page, so any creator with "Custom tip page" ticked can have it later: the header block has three new settings, `frame` (none, flame, aura, slash, custom), `bannerFx` (none, embers, slashes, shimmer, custom) and two file ids `frameAsset` and `bannerFxAsset` (a picture or GIF of the page library, same upload checks and the same Remove protection, Replace and take-down handling as the banner picture). The ready-made looks are CSS in the page accent colour (`globals.css` end, `src/components/tip/Decor.tsx`, `src/lib/page-doc/decor.ts`); reduced motion stops the rings and hides the drifting lights; a custom GIF shows its still unless the device may play motion. An effect with no banner picture gets its own banner strip. Edited in the owner's page editor under Header (`BlockSettings.tsx`, a file picker shows only when "Your own picture or GIF" is chosen). `setAsset` takes an optional place name; `usedAssetIds`, `replaceAssetId` and `asTemplate` know the two new ids. Default is off for every creator. The owner's own page (AcePressure, `ownerPageDoc()` in `src/lib/page-doc/defaults.ts`) now starts with the "Clutch pulse" banner and no ring over the photo (the owner disliked the ring over the logo and asked for something more creative: a heart-monitor line that spikes, a flash and a crosshair that locks on at the spike, one 3.6 s loop in the page's crimson, `clutch` in `decor.ts`, `Decor.tsx` and `globals.css`; the flame ring and the embers stay available in the editor; built for AcePressure first, other creators later on request); if a custom page is published for the owner's account, that page's own header settings win, and they are changed in the editor. Checked: type check, lint, build, the page-document tests (new `test/page-decor.spec.ts`, old tests updated for the new header fields) and screenshots of all three frames and effects in Chrome from the fixture page. Not yet seen: the editor with a real login, a real upload of a frame picture, Safari and Firefox, a real phone.

## Current state (2026-11-04, PayU purchase, Part 2 step B built: the Buy form, the message after paying, the receipt, the reminder and the refund wording; NO new migration, 0055 from step A still to run BEFORE the deploy)

- **Built (asked for, , owner's choice).** `/dashboard/plans` now has the Buy form (`BuyPass.tsx`) and the server action `src/app/actions/plan-checkout.ts`: the creator picks 1 or 12 months, types a 10-digit mobile number (PayU requires it; passed on, not stored) and ticks the terms box; the server checks all of it again, makes the order with the amount fixed on our side and returns PayU's signed form, which the browser posts to PayU. The form appears only when prices are set, `PAYU_KEY` and `PAYU_SALT` are set, and (test mode) the creator is the owner; otherwise "Buying opens soon" or "The price will be announced soon" as before, so **nothing changes for creators until the owner gives prices.**
- **Rules added, each with a test (`test/plan-buy.spec.ts`, 19):** the price shown is the price charged (`quotePlan`, `expectAmountMinor`: if the last early seat went, the order is refused with `price_changed` and nobody is sent to PayU); a bad mobile number or email is refused before any order exists (`checkPayer`, split out of `checkoutForm`); the message after PayU sends the creator back comes from the database (`paymentNotice`: a typed `?payment=paid` with no paid order is never a success; PayU's own error text is never shown), and a pending order makes the page ask again by itself for about 40 seconds (`PendingRefresh`); **Your payments** lists paid orders with a **Receipt** (`/dashboard/plans/receipt/<id>`: a plain receipt, not a tax invoice, nothing about GST per `docs/rules-check.md` section 2, only the paying creator can open it, a test payment says so, print or save as PDF); the last 5 days of a pass show "Your paid features end on ..." on the page, and the daily job (`/api/cron/plan-orders`, third job) emails the creator once per end date (`pass_reminded_until`, claimed before sending and given back when the send fails or is off; needs `RESEND_API_KEY` and `CONTACT_FROM_EMAIL` on a Resend-verified domain, otherwise "reminders off" in the cron reply and the on-screen note only).
- **Refund wording (owner chose "fix errors only", 2026-11-04):** a pass is not refunded once its features are on; only payment errors are put right (a switch-on is repaired or the money returned the way it was paid). Written in the terms (a "Paid features" bullet in the Fees section, no renumbering), the refund policy (new "Paid feature passes" section; the old "There is no subscription" line is reworded), the privacy policy (the pass record, the mobile number goes to PayU and is not stored, the reminder email; the Resend line in `PROCESSORS`), the pricing page ("Optional paid features", no price) and the Buy form's tick box (also checked on the server). The lines "no monthly fee" and "no monthly plan" are gone because they would stop being true. `LEGAL_UPDATED` is now 4 November 2026. Not read by a lawyer.
- **Verified:** `tsc`, `eslint`, `npm run build`; unit run: `test/plan-buy.spec.ts` (19), `test/plan-orders.spec.ts` and `test/payu.spec.ts` all pass; the signed-out route tests (receipt included) pass; **a new browser spec, `test/plan-buy-form.spec.ts` (7 tests on a made-up fixture page `/dev-fixtures/plans`, 404 unless `PAGE_FIXTURES=1`), passes in real Chrome on a desktop and a Pixel 5 profile**: the Pay button needs a 10-digit number and the tick and shows the chosen term's price, early and renewing wording, pressing Pay with no signed-in creator gives a plain message and turns the button back on, the messages read plainly, no sideways scroll at 320 and 390 px; I looked at a 390 px screenshot. ## Current state (2026-11-04, PayU purchase, Part 2 step A built, reviewed by three independent reviewers and fixed; run migration 0055 BEFORE the deploy)

- **Built (then an independent review, all verified findings fixed); the Buy button does not exist, so nothing can create an order and no prices are set.** Migration `0055_plan_orders.sql` (table `plan_orders`; `users.pass_keys`, `pass_until`, `pass_reminded_until`). `src/lib/payu.ts` (settings, request and reply hashes, the checkout form, PayU's Verify Payment call; pure, the network call passed in). `src/lib/plan-orders.ts` (`chooseAmount`, `createPlanOrder`, `settlePlanPayment`, `reconcilePendingOrders`, `expirePasses`, `writeFeatureKeys`). `src/lib/plan-settle.ts` and the routes `/api/payu/return`, `/api/payu/cancel`, `/api/webhooks/payu`, `/api/cron/plan-orders` (daily 18:05 UTC, in `vercel.json` and the Health page, with a PayU variables checklist). `PASS_FEATURE_KEYS` in `src/lib/plan-catalogue.ts`. Facts, sources and the reasons for each rule in `docs/payu-notes.md`.
- **The rules, each with a test:** a payment is believed only when the reply hash matches (any of PayU's four documented layouts, salt needed), the amount, our order id (udf1) and the mode agree, and PayU's Verify Payment API says captured at the plain price (a discount or differing amounts is "look at by hand", never a success) and the payment id comes from PayU's answer, not the form; pending to paid happens once with both rows locked in the same transaction as the switch-on; a failure only fills a pending order and a failed one can still be paid; one PayU payment id pays one order; a pass remembers the keys it added so expiry never removes the owner's ticks; months are added in UTC with the month-end clamp; early seats are counted by creator (a holder keeps the early price when renewing up to 31 days after the end, an unpaid early order holds a seat two hours, a lapsed holder's seat is not handed on); test money only from the owner's account; five unpaid orders per creator per hour. The Buy form's values are checked against PayU's limits before signing (name 20, email 50, return addresses https and 50, a 10-digit mobile).
- **Fixes from the review:** the Verify check is asked twice and a lagging PayU gives "try again" (webhook 503), never a wrong failure; the daily job asks only about the server's own mode, newest first, in a 40 second budget, one bad order cannot stop the rest, orders PayU never saw are closed after a day (still payable by a late notice); the owner's feature box refuses a save from a stale page; bodies are read with a size cap and only as a form; the finance page's turnover now includes pass sales; the migration header says what works before it is run.
- **Verified:** `tsc`, `eslint`, build; `test/payu.spec.ts` (20) and `test/plan-orders.spec.ts` (39) plus the PayU route tests, the finance spec, the pinned guard and the Health guard: 85 passed locally (the 12 public-route page tests need a browser that is not installed here and run in CI). The lock races need a real database and were not shown (one-connection test database). **Not tried against PayU at all:** no real or test payment yet.
- **Accepted limits (in `docs/payu-notes.md`):** a refund or dispute is only logged, the owner handles it by hand; a renewal adds the pass keys again, including one the owner unticked in between; a feature built later does not reach a running pass; nothing stops a creator with a running pass from closing their account.
- **To confirm on the first test payment:** the maximum length of `txnid` (we use 20 letters and digits), that the browser return arrives as a form POST on `/api/payu/return`, what PayU does when the webhook answers an error, whether PayU rewrites the name or email it echoes, which of the four hash layouts it signs with, whether the Verify API lags its own notice.
- **Next:** step B (medium): Buy button and mobile-number field on `/dashboard/plans`, the `?payment=` message there (read from the database, never trusted from the address), the reminder 5 days before a pass ends, a plain receipt, refund wording in the terms and a privacy line about the mobile number, then the owner's test payment. Owner to do now: set `PAYU_KEY`, `PAYU_SALT` and `PAYU_MODE=test` in Vercel (never in chat), run 0055, and create the PayU dashboard webhook (`OPERATIONS.md` 7i).

## Earlier state (2026-11-03, per-creator fee rates)

- **Built; run migration 0054 BEFORE the deploy.** The owner can set the fee rates: a site-wide pair (default 5% platform fee and 2.75% processing charge, `/admin/fees`), an optional pair per creator (the **Fee rates** box in `/admin/creators/<id>`) and a bulk "apply to the ticked creators" in `/admin/fees` (search, filter, tick, apply or put back on the site rates, up to 500 at a time). The owner's own account `acepressure` is set to 0% and 0% by the migration (once, audited). Up to two decimals; the ceilings are 30% and 10%.
- **How it works.** `src/lib/money.ts` `splitTip(gross, rates)`, `feeLines` and `checkRates` take a pair (standard when none is given; `RULE_VERSION` is now `2026-11-03.1`). `src/lib/fee-rates.ts` reads and words rates and resolves the layers (standard, site in `site_settings`, a creator's own in `users.platform_bps_override` and `processing_bps_override`, both or neither). `createTipOrder` resolves the pair before the Razorpay order and copies it onto the order (`tip_orders.platform_bps`, `processing_bps`, written only when not the standard pair, so a standard-rate creator still tips before the migration is run). The books (`accrual.ts`), the dashboard estimates, the tips list, the CSV and the recap read the pair saved on each tip, so a change of rate never reaches an earlier tip. A pair outside the allowed range stops the posting loudly (the sweep retries). Saving is in `src/lib/fee-admin.ts` (audit rows in the same transaction: `creator.fee_rates_changed`, `site.fee_rates_changed`); the actions are in `src/app/actions/fees.ts`, owner-only (a source test checks each starts with the owner check).
- **Checked here:** type check and lint pass; `npm run build` passes (`/admin/fees` is a route); 25 new specs in `test/fee-rates.spec.ts` (money maths with a pair, reading typed percentages, the three layers, the books at no fees and at own rates, an old tip keeping its rate after changes, bulk saves and their audit rows, the migration run twice, the owner-only guard) and the money, ledger, earnings, balances, finance, creator and admin specs pass (183 in all, run without the browser server because another process held port 3300). A real bug the tests caught: the first version of the "both numbers or neither" database check let a half-set pair through (NULL passes a CHECK); fixed before it was committed.
- **Not yet seen:** the Fees page and the Fee rates box in a browser with a real owner login; a real tip at no fees through Razorpay and the books; the dashboard wording for a creator at no fees. The browser suite was not run.
- **Open for the owner:** the public pages still say "5% of every tip" (that is the standard rate); with custom rates the wording needs a decision. The earlier owner note that "the fee must stay flat" for a paid plan is now overtaken by this request; per-creator rates are private to the owner console and the creator's own dashboard. A real split by the payment provider is a separate step that waits for the provider answer (Razorpay Route, Cashfree Easy Split; PayU's split product was not researched): these rates are what it would use.

## Earlier state (2026-11-03, polish pass after the page builder)

- **Done (five independent reviewers in parallel, then the safe fixes applied by hand); NO migration, nothing to run.** Audits: tip page and page builder, OBS overlays and the overlay editor, creator dashboard, owner admin, and a competitor re-check on public pages. Authorisation across the admin (every page, server action and upload route) came back clean; paid-switch gating on every dashboard page and save action came back clean.
- **OBS overlays.** The alert box now polls with `?controls=1` while an alert waits, so Skip and Test are seen at once (before, Skip with one alert showing and one waiting cleared the wrong one); the route answers that with the commands only and leases nothing. A 404 no longer stops the alert box for the rest of the stream: it shows the banner, asks once a minute and recovers (like the goal bar). New `src/app/overlay/error.tsx` (draws nothing, retries after 5 s) so a render error never puts the site's error page over a stream. Overlay pages no longer run the smooth-scroll loop (`SmoothScroll` returns on `/overlay`) or the sign-in check (`src/components/auth/Providers.tsx` skips `SessionProvider` under `/overlay/<token>`; the dashboard's `/overlay/preview` keeps it because it has the nav). Not done: jitter and backoff on the pollers, `?kind=` on the supporters route, `color-scheme` reset, the individual `translate:` and `scale:` properties that need Chromium 104 (OBS 28 to 30 may be older: check the OBS version creators run; on the try list).
- **Editors (admin).** The "send the waiting change on leaving" effect now keeps `save` in a ref with `[]` deps in both `PageEditor` and `OverlayEditor` (a re-render after Publish gave the action a new identity and fired a stray save that could cause a false "changed somewhere else"). Overlay editor: the preview style is memoised (every autosave used to queue another sample alert), "Swap with previous version" appears right after the first Publish and is disabled while a change is unsaved.
- **Tip page.** Streamer picks follow the same motion rules as the backdrop (still icon on reduced motion, data saver or a slow network); small accent-coloured text is now `text-text` (the owner red fails contrast for small text); helper text uses `text-muted`, not `text-control`; the disabled Tip button says why ("Tick the box above to send your tip.", linked with `aria-describedby`); Razorpay's script loads when the box is ticked; the loading skeleton uses the real page's wrappers (no jump); bio and the success heading wrap long strings at 320px; the Pressure meter poll cannot start a second chain when the tab returns; the owner's brand name and red reach the WhatsApp share image and the highlights page, and `NotReady` uses the display name; dead owner-page CSS removed (`.ap-strip`, `.ap-hero`, `.ap-bust`, `.ap-ring`, `ap-end`); the pin line reads "Available on tips of ₹X or more, at no extra fee." Because of the new line and the label colour, the four ARIA structure snapshots in `test/snapshots` were re-recorded on purpose (only those two lines changed) and the local pixel baseline was re-recorded after looking at the diff (only the labels, the pin line, the new line and the footer shift).
- **Dashboard.** A creator whose Streamlabs switch is off but who still has a saved connection can open `/dashboard/streamlabs` and disconnect (only that button); with nothing stored it still goes to the overview. Timestamps: Disconnect YouTube, Disconnect Discord, Remove the link and Remove the token each ask first, and the page refreshes in place instead of reloading (half-typed fields survive). Banner: the photo buttons answer in their own tile. Pinned: Save answers under Save, the list actions in the list tile, and the stale "about N s left" is gone. Rules: a hint says why Save is greyed. Developer text (`run db/migrations/... in Neon`) no longer shows to creators; the log keeps the migration name. `getSessionFeatures` is wrapped in React `cache()` (the nav, sub nav and page asked three times).
- **Admin.** `approveHandleRequest` now finishes a request whose rename already happened (a crash between the rename and the mark left it pending forever; new test in `test/handle.spec.ts`). Reset to pending asks first (with an optional reason). The creator id has the int32 upper bound everywhere (404 instead of a database error). `/admin/media` copy no longer says first uploads wait for approval; its images load lazily and videos do not preload; the `mailto:` keeps a plain `@`. `OPERATIONS.md` 7g says the three migrations to run and that Publish is refused for a taken-down picture.
- **Competitor re-check** (public pages only): `docs/competitor-findings.md` has the 2026-11-03 section. Short version: five new competitors, the 7.75% total is behind the 0 to 5% direct-UPI and subscription models and ahead of the 10% sites, no public copy claims "lowest cost" (keep it that way).
- **Verified:** `tsc`, `eslint`, `npm run build`; unit specs 943 passed (the browser-dependent ones cannot launch Playwright's Chromium here, as before); `test/handle.spec.ts` 13 of 13; browser specs on installed Chrome through a temporary config (desktop and Pixel 5): the whole suite 2450 passed, 2 skipped, none failed (the 14 `waitlist-rules` rows marked ✘ are the existing `test.fail()` markers for the known three-character `@x` channel bug, not failures); the pixel gate 12 of 12 after the re-record. **Not verified:** any of this with a real login, a real phone, real Razorpay or real OBS (the new checks are in `docs/owner-try-list.md`); the iPhone Safari project; the alert box's controls-only polling against a real Neon database (the route change is two lines and the existing overlay specs pass).
- **Still open from the audits (not done on purpose or not yet):** (1) the dashboard nav has 14 links with every switch on and only about five show on sub pages (needs the owner's call: group the studio pages like `AlertSubNav`, or fade the edges with scroll buttons); (2) names do not match between nav, headings and buttons (Look / Alert style / volume; Words and motion / Alert text; Tip page picks / rule; Automatic tiers / variation); (3) [done 2026-11-04] switched-off pages for Timestamps, Media and Banner now open with only the remove buttons while something is stored; (4) alert studio forms (Text, Motion, Layout, Style) lose unsaved edits on a tab click and keep a stale "Saved."; (5) chip groups use `role="radio"` without arrow keys (use `ChipRadio`); (6) the sticky preview takes a third of a 320 px screen; (7) admin: no audit rows for page asset add and remove, discard draft, Sync, message handled, voice words; publish takes whatever draft is stored with no base check; no undo in the page editor; the "copy from" picker lists only 200 creators; the "Owed to creators" card nets creators who owe us against those owed; (8) tip page: the success path (GSAP, Odometer) is in the first-load bundle, the amount well changes height as digits are typed, "₹1,00,000" spills at 320 px, the Odometer has no digit grouping, no `noscript` note, about ten repeated `users` reads per tip-page view, heavy blur on the owner backdrop on phones, `DESIGN.md` does not describe the page builder and owner page pieces, a clip-helper step promises a download that does not exist.
- **Owner decisions needed:** (a) the Send box says "final and non-refundable once shown on stream", which implies a refund before it is shown and conflicts with the pin and refund wording; suggested "final and non-refundable (payment errors are still put right)"; (b) "Tip again" keeps the box ticked, while the last commit says never pre-ticked: should it clear per tip? (c) drop the reduced-motion media query inside OBS only (it follows the streamer's OS animation setting and can turn a paid bounce into a fade)? (d) stop alerts for a suspended creator (the alert and goal routes do not check `creator_status`, the other overlay routes do)? (e) skip polling while an OBS source is hidden (a hidden scene can consume and lose an alert), unverified in OBS? (f) the invalid-link banner on a live stream: keep, shrink or silence? (g) approve tips from Discord or Telegram (the one competitor feature worth considering)? (h) add the page builder and owner page to `DESIGN.md` as a named exception, or flatten them?

## Earlier state (2026-11-03, page builder phase 4 built: the Pressure meter)

- **Page builder phase 4 built ( for the build; the owner said "go as planned"); NO migration to run; nothing a fan sees changes until the owner switches on "Pressure meter" for a creator, adds the block in the editor and publishes.** Plan: `docs/plans/2026-11-03-page-builder-phase-4-pressure-meter.md`. **What it does:** a creator's goal (daily, weekly, monthly or never repeating, whichever the creator set under Goal bars) drawn as a bar with rank words, as a block on the tip page and as an OBS overlay. New feature switch `pressureMeter` (Tip page group; needs "Custom tip page", so it is off if either is off). New block type `pressureMeter` in the page document (left column, under the header and links, before the alert preview); it is in no default page and is added from the editor ("Add a Pressure meter", offered only while the creator's switch is on). Settings: which goal (the first goal by default), rank words as percent of the goal (a second, separate ladder from the amount box's; default the One Piece words), rupees or percent only, tip page and/or OBS, mark the goal-reached moment, bar colour (must show against the page ground, 3 to 1) and a heading (standard "Pressure"). **The meter has no period or money logic of its own:** every number comes from `loadGoalProgress`, the goal bar's engine (`src/lib/page-doc/pressure.ts` does the database half, `meter-reading.ts` the pure half the browser also uses), so the two agree; the words "today", "this week", "this month" follow the goal's own period and are never typed. **Tip page:** the first reading is in the served HTML (one `loadGoalProgress` only when the block is on the page, visible, set for the page and both switches are on), then the block polls `GET /api/pressure/<handle>` every 30 seconds while the tab is visible (not in the editor preview, and not at all if the creator had no goal on load). The endpoint is public and read-only, 404 unless both switches are on and the published page has a visible meter set for the page, cached 10 seconds at the edge (`s-maxage=10`), with a small in-memory per-network limit (60 a minute, `src/lib/window-limit.ts`; **deviation from the plan, which said "as the contact form does": that limiter writes a row per call, which a poll must not do**). **Percent only** leaves the rupee figures out of the endpoints' responses and the served HTML altogether, not just off the screen. **OBS:** `/overlay/<alert token>/pressure` (the alert link plus `/pressure`; the dashboard lists it with the other extra overlays while the switch is on); polls `/api/overlay/<token>/pressure` every 5 s (once a minute after a 404), follows the published page's settings, flashes once when it watches the bar cross 100% (not when it loads full), draws nothing with no goal, a hidden meter, "page only" or a switch off. **Editor:** "Add a Pressure meter" (a note says what to do when the switch is off), a settings panel (goal picker from the creator's goals, rank words in percent, show, where, celebration, colour, heading) with plain notes when the page will show nothing and why, the OBS link written out, and a preview that works the reading out from the live draft and the creator's real goals (so adding the meter or changing its goal shows at once). **Publish refuses** a draft with a meter for a creator whose meter switch is off. **Left out on purpose:** a picture or animation on the meter (the spec lists it; one more place for assets and its own motion rules; later), and a stretch goal.
- **Checked here (2026-11-03):** lint, type check and the production build pass; unit specs: 15 for the reading and goal choice (`test/pressure.spec.ts`, including period edges across a Sunday to Monday in India, a daily goal at its midnight, another creator's goal id ignored, a deleted goal falling back) and 11 for the endpoints and the in-memory limiter (`test/pressure-routes.spec.ts`: both switches, hidden, closed account, the other place, bad handles, percent only with no amount anywhere in the JSON), the parser, edit functions and field table (`test/page-doc.spec.ts`, `page-doc-edit.spec.ts`, `page-doc-fields.spec.ts`) and the publish refusal (`test/page-doc-store.spec.ts`); 18 browser specs on desktop Chrome (`test/pressure-ui.spec.ts`: first reading in the served HTML, percent only, goal reached, hidden and OBS-only, position under the header on the owner page, 320/390/1440 px with a 60-letter title, the 30 second poll and a 404, no polling in the editor preview, the OBS bar, the one-off flash, a vertical stage) and 7 editor specs (`test/page-editor.spec.ts`, "Pressure meter"); **the phase 1 "identical to today" gate still passes 12 of 12** (default and owner pages have no meter block, so nothing moves). The whole desktop browser suite (installed Chrome, temporary config) also passes: 1128 passed, 2 skipped, none failed.
- **Not verified (needs the owner):** the meter on a real phone with the goal at 0, 50 and 100%, a real tip moving the bar within 30 seconds on the page and within 5 in OBS, the OBS source in real OBS (the flash, the vertical canvas), the edge cache (`s-maxage=10`) on Vercel, the editor against Neon with a real login and real goals, a daily goal over midnight in India, WebKit (iPhone), Lighthouse with the meter on. The reading was tested against an in-memory Postgres only.
- **Owner to do:** deploy (nothing to migrate), open `/admin/creators/<id>`, tick "Pressure meter" beside "Custom tip page" for a test creator who has a goal, open the editor on a laptop, "Add a Pressure meter", pick the goal, change a word, Publish, look at the page on a phone, add `/pressure` to that creator's alert link as a Browser source in OBS and send a small test tip. Steps in `OPERATIONS.md` 7g item 10; things to look at in `docs/owner-try-list.md`.
- **Next:** nothing planned in the page builder until the owner has tried phases 2 to 4. Phase 5 (later): OBS overlays (alert, goal) in the same builder, copy a page as a template, a picture or animation on the meter. The independent review of phase 4 (plan task 8) was run 2026-11-03 (four independent reviewers): no high-severity bug. Fixed: Publish no longer refuses a page whose meter is only hidden while the meter switch is off (there is no remove action, so it used to lock Publish); `goalPercent` now uses integer maths (290 of 1000 showed 28; the goal bar gets the same fix); the meter bar shows a border in forced-colours mode. Left as known notes: the 10 s edge cache can be bypassed with a query string (the in-memory limit is the only brake, per serverless instance); a deleted or foreign goal id falls back silently to the creator's first goal (the plan's choice); a 404 stops tip page polling until reload; two polls can overlap after a tab switch (cosmetic); duplicate rank names are allowed; no direct tests of the route cache headers; restoring the previous version has no meter check (fans are safe, rendering is gated by the switches).

## Earlier state (2026-11-03, page builder phase 3 built: pictures and videos)

- **Page builder phase 3 built (effort as the session ran, ; the owner said "go ahead, treat it as your own website", so the choices below are mine and any can change); run migration 0052 BEFORE the deploy (the tip page, the editor and `/admin/media` still open without it and say so); nothing a fan sees changes until the owner publishes a page that uses a picture.** Plan: `docs/plans/2026-11-03-page-builder-phase-3-assets.md`. **What it does:** a table `page_assets` (migration `0052_page_assets.sql`) holds the files the owner uploads for a creator's tip page (pictures, GIFs, WebM videos; Blob folder `page/<creatorId>/`; owner only). The page document stores only an id: the **backdrop** block gained `style` ("ember" the owner's smoke, or "media"), `asset` and `dim` (0 to 80, how dark a layer of the page colour sits over the picture, 40 to start), and the **header** block gained `bannerAsset` (a picture or GIF in place of the creator's own banner; no video). The editor: **Add a picture or video behind the page** at the top of the block list (a page without a backdrop gets a "media" one), a picture picker with the library, **Upload a new file**, **Replace**, **Remove**, a darkening slider and live preview (the preview frame loads again when the library changes). Rules: picture or GIF up to 3 MB, WebM up to 8 MB and 15 s, at most 4096 px, 10 files and 40 MB per page; the server re-checks the first bytes of the file and of its still picture; animated files must come with a still picture of their first frame made in the browser (a file whose still cannot be made is refused); the upload token is owner only and only for `page/<id>/` of a creator whose switch is on. Fans get the still first in the served page; a GIF or video starts only after the page has loaded and not when the device asks for reduced motion, is in data-saver mode, is on 2G or 3G, or reports 2 GB of memory or less (`src/lib/page-doc/motion.ts`, `use-motion.ts`, `BackdropMedia.tsx`). `/admin/media` has a second list, "Tip page pictures", with Take down (erases the files even while a page uses them; the page then shows its normal look). The daily orphan job also cleans `page/`.
- **Choices made without asking (the owner may change any).** (1) **Replace goes through Publish, unlike the spec** (the spec said replace keeps the id, so a replaced file would change the live page at once, skipping Publish and Revert): Replace uploads a new file and points the draft at it everywhere; the old file stays (and "Swap with previous version" still shows it) until the owner removes it. (2) **Remove is refused while the draft, the published page or the previous version uses the file**, and says which. (3) A **media backdrop with no usable file draws nothing** (a plain page), not the smoke, so a normal creator never gets the owner's red embers. (4) **Only the backdrop and the header banner take pictures**; a picture on the thank-you screen, a logo block and a free image block are left out. (5) **WebM only, no MP4**: a browser that cannot play WebM shows the still. (6) Replacing a file used as both backdrop and banner with a video leaves the banner on the creator's own banner. (7) "Used by the draft" in the picker follows the draft on screen; the server's refusal uses the saved draft, so a Remove within 2 seconds of taking a picture off a block can be refused once and then works.
- **Checked here (2026-11-03):** type check, lint and the production build pass; 34 pure specs (`test/page-assets.spec.ts`: rules, parser, edit functions, motion rules) and 20 database specs on an in-memory Postgres (`test/page-assets-store.spec.ts`: migration, unique files, publish refused for a missing picture, allowance, another creator's file never resolved, removal refused while used, double remove, take-down, audit rows, missing table); 18 browser specs against the real page through the fixture route (`test/page-assets-page.spec.ts`: picture backdrop and dimming, missing file, no video in the served HTML, video after load, GIF swap, reduced motion, data saver, 3G, 2 GB device, banner, 320 px) and 7 editor specs (`test/page-editor.spec.ts`, "pictures"), all run in installed Chrome with the temporary-config method; the earlier page specs (128 in all with these) pass; **the phase 1 "identical to today" gate passes (12 of 12: pixels at 320, 390 and 1280, tab order, served text) against the baseline recorded before page builder phase 1.** The full unit config's remaining failures are the browser specs that need Chromium or a running site (same as before).
- **Independent review (a fresh reviewer read the diff against the plan's risk list) and what it changed.** Fixed: an animated WebP or PNG passed as a still picture and ignored the motion rules, so the server (and the browser first) now refuse them ("save it as a GIF or WebM"), posters too; JPEG and WebP sizes are now read from the file on the server instead of trusted from the browser; the same file could be saved twice and a retry could delete the first save's file, so migration 0052 now has unique indexes on the file and still addresses and the actions never delete a file a live row points at; a taken-down file whose delete failed was never retried, so the daily job now treats only live rows as in use and the take-down message says when the file was not erased; Remove is disabled in the editor for a file the draft on screen uses, and Publish is refused when the draft names a picture that is no longer a live file of that creator; Replace now skips a place that cannot take the new file's kind (a video never becomes a banner) and says so if no place can; the header block only listens for the motion rules when its banner is a GIF. **Not changed (known limits):** a Blob address is trusted when it is on any `*.public.blob.vercel-storage.com` host under `page/<id>/` (the owner's own session would have to craft a URL to use another store; pinning the store id from the token was not verified here); a still-image backdrop is a plain `<img>` up to 3 MB with no priority hint; the darkening can be set to 0%.
- **Not verified (needs the owner):** a real Blob upload end to end (the editor's upload goes through `/api/page-assets/upload` and `@vercel/blob/client`; the fixture editor replaces uploads with a refusal), a real WebM or GIF made by an AI tool (does the browser make the still, is the size under the caps), the page on a real phone and a real iPhone (does WebM play, does the still show), a real data-saver or low-memory device, Lighthouse for a page that uses a backdrop (the gate covers only the two default pages), the editor with a real login against Neon, migration 0052 against Neon.
- **Owner to do:** run 0052, deploy, tick "Custom tip page" for a test creator, open the editor on a laptop, add a backdrop, upload a picture and a short WebM, check the dimming in the preview, Publish, open the page on a phone (also with reduced motion or data saver on), Replace the picture, Swap with previous version, Remove the unused file, try Take down in `/admin/media`. Steps in `OPERATIONS.md` 7g item 9.
- **Next:** phase 4, the Pressure meter (block, OBS overlay, goal-reached moment; design in `docs/specs/2026-11-03-page-builder-design.md`). 

## Earlier state (2026-11-03, page builder phase 2 built: the editor)

- **Page builder phase 2 built ( for the build); no migration to run (0051 from phase 1 is all it needs); nothing a fan sees changes until the owner publishes a change.** The owner-only editor is at `/admin/creators/<id>/page`, opened by the **Edit tip page** button in the "Custom tip page" box of the creator's detail page (shown once the `pageBuilder` switch is on). What it does: block list (move with arrows or drag, hide with the eye; header, amount, note and send are protected and cannot be hidden), a settings panel per block (every word a block may change, the rank ladder under the amount bar, page colours with a contrast check that keeps the owner red `#ca1b19` allowed), a live preview that is the real page in a phone or laptop width frame (an owner-only route, `/page-preview/<id>`, fed the draft by `postMessage`; clicking a piece of the preview selects its block), a draft that autosaves after 1.5 s with a compare-and-swap (a second tab gets a conflict message and cannot overwrite), **Publish** (stores the cleaned document, keeps the old one as "previous"; refuses a draft that is already published), **Revert to published**, a warning when closing with unsaved changes, and "open on a laptop" on narrow screens. Step numbers on the form follow the order the blocks sit. The console box now says Custom / Normal / Unreadable (fans see the normal page), shows the time of the last change (read from the audit log) and calls Restore "Swap with previous version". The creator's dashboard shows one line while their switch is on: "Your custom tip page is set up by Freetip. To change it, send a message through the contact form." (no editor for creators). Locked, never editable: the message limit, minimum tip, fee wording, "tips are final", the anonymous option, the name field's behaviour, what Pay does and the amount on the Pay button. Plan: `docs/plans/2026-11-03-page-builder-phase-2-editor.md`. **Checked (2026-11-03, final verification):** lint, `tsc` and `npm run build` pass; the whole browser suite on desktop Chrome and the Pixel 5 profile passed (1962 passed, 2 skipped, before the review fixes below; the editor, preview, block and paid-feature specs were re-run after them on both profiles, 174 passed); the page-doc unit specs (115) pass; the phase 1 pixel and tab-order gate is 12 of 12 against the old-code baseline; Lighthouse (mobile preset, local production build, fixture pages, three runs each) owner 84 performance and 97 accessibility, normal page 85 and 97, the same as before phase 2. **Independent review (three fresh read-only reviewers: security and access, store and editing logic, fan page and editor UI):** no high-severity finding; the security reviewer found every action and route owner-gated, the preview messages origin- and window-checked, the framing exception limited to the preview paths, and no way for a creator to write their own page. Fixed after review, each with a failing test first (`test/page-editor-review.spec.ts`): the preview could stop following edits if the frame's first "ready" message was lost (the editor now offers the draft until the frame answers); Publish could send a different draft than the one on screen (changing anything closes the Publish question); Revert could race a save in flight (it is off while saving); a change made in the last moment before leaving the editor by a link inside the site was lost (it is sent on leaving); an owner-console id above the database's integer range gave an error instead of a 404; the move buttons no longer lose keyboard focus at the top or bottom of a section. **Known and left as they are (low):** after Publish or Revert the keyboard focus is not put anywhere in particular and the result line may not be announced by every screen reader; the ladder and colour messages are not tied to their fields with `aria-describedby`; an identical refusal is not announced twice; the button-word field accepts digits and "₹" (the amount still shows after it); a custom page colour between 3:1 and 4.5:1 can make the small accent-coloured "Deselect the pick" link fall under 4.5:1 (the 3:1 rule is the plan's choice); the dashboard note appears as soon as the switch is on, before a page is published; if a save reaches the server but its reply is lost, the next save reports "Changed somewhere else" and a reload fixes it; a double-submitted Swap swaps twice; the audit row for publish, swap and reset records no actor (same as the other admin actions). **Not yet seen:** the real owner login and the editor against Neon (the jsonb compare-and-swap was tested only on in-memory Postgres), the frame header on Vercel, the editor on a real laptop, the live pages on a real phone, WebKit (iPhone) runs. **Owner to do:** deploy (nothing to migrate), tick "Custom tip page" for a test creator, open the editor on a laptop, change a word and a colour, wait for "Draft saved", Publish, look at the page on a phone, press Swap with previous version, then Reset to normal page. Steps in `OPERATIONS.md` 7g. Next: phase 3, assets (upload, replace, remove; animated backgrounds), planned after the owner has tried the editor.

## Earlier state (2026-11-03, page builder phase 1 built: the foundation)

- **Page builder phase 1 built; run migration 0051 BEFORE the deploy (the tip page also loads without it); the switch "Custom tip page" (`pageBuilder`) is off for everyone and there is no publish or editor yet, so nothing a fan sees changes.** Built natively in one session (effort) from `docs/plans/2026-11-03-page-builder-phase-1-foundation.md`. **What it is:** every tip page, the owner's `acepressure` page and every creator's, is now drawn from a checked document of blocks (backdrop, header, links, alert preview, QR, amount, note, send, strip, sign-off). `src/lib/page-doc/` holds the document types, the block table, one lenient parser (a bad stored document gives the normal page; a page missing its amount, note or Send block gets them put back, so a fan can always pay), the two default documents that reproduce today's normal and owner pages (`defaultPageDoc()`, `ownerPageDoc()`: the owner's red, tagline, Instagram, ranks and strip now live there instead of in `owner-page.ts`), the rank ladder, the theme and the database store (publish, restore, reset, each audited). `src/components/tip/blocks/` has one component per block and the layout; `TipForm.tsx` keeps all the state and the whole payment code and only its returned JSX moved (the `variant` branches are gone, one rendering path). Migration `0051_page_builder.sql` adds `page_draft`, `page_published`, `page_previous` to `users`. The real page reads the stored document only while the creator's switch is on (`getPageRow` in `creator.ts`, `resolvePageDoc`); the owner console (`/admin/creators/<id>`) has a "Custom tip page" box under Paid features with Restore previous version and Reset to normal page (`src/app/actions/page-doc.ts`; each asks twice and writes an audit row). `/dev-fixtures/tip` is a test-only copy of the tip page with made-up data (404 unless `PAGE_FIXTURES=1`, set only by the Playwright config, never in Vercel). Setup and use: `OPERATIONS.md` 7g. **Verified:** lint, type check and build pass; new specs pass (33 for the document, parser, defaults, rank ladder and theme, 19 for the store against an in-memory Postgres, 6 for the owner page, 2 for the fixture switch) together with the paid-features and foundation specs (88 in the unit run); 25 browser tests in `test/tip-page-blocks.spec.ts` (on each browser profile) prove the document decides the page (hiding the QR, a strip on any page, custom rank words, no ladder, a hidden Send block still paying, the Instagram link built from a plain handle only, the sign-off style, a document that is not a page giving the page's own default); committed text snapshots of today's pages (recorded from the old code before it was changed) match; **the gate** (pixels at 320, 390 and 1280 px, tab order, both success screens and the text in the served HTML, compared with a baseline recorded from the old code, tolerance 0.2 percent) passes 12 of 12 for the normal and the owner page, and was shown able to fail (hiding one QR tile gives 3 percent different pixels); Lighthouse (mobile, three runs each, medians) is exactly as before: owner 84 performance and 97 accessibility, normal page 85 and 97; the whole browser suite on desktop Chrome and a mobile Chrome profile: 1,742 passed, 2 skipped, none failed (the 14 marked failed in the list are existing tests that expect a known bug to still be there). **Independent review:** a fresh reviewer read the whole branch and found nothing critical; the payment code in `TipForm.tsx` is byte-identical to before apart from `variant` becoming `doc`. One real defect: the longest words the parser allows (a 60-letter tagline, 40-letter strip words, a 24-letter rank name, no spaces) made the page scroll sideways at 320 px; fixed by letting those texts break anywhere (`OwnerExtras.tsx`), with a test that failed first (the page was 870 px wide) and passes on both profiles; the pixel gate is unchanged (12 of 12), Lighthouse was not re-run after this CSS-only change. **For the phase 2 plan (from the review, not built):** the owner decides whether the `header` block should be protected like amount, note and send (today a document can hide it, which removes the creator name and the page heading); the colour check should also keep text readable against the page ground (the owner red must stay allowed); publishing should store the checked document, not the raw draft, and tell the owner what the parser dropped; step numbers 1, 2, 3 are fixed in the three step blocks and need to follow a reordered page; Restore is a toggle (a double click or a second tab silently undoes it) and the console box says "Custom" even for an unreadable stored document; a ladder whose top is at or below the minimum tip should hide the bar; the OG image and the creator's own accent setting ignore a document colour; the block context hands raw setters to every block and should be narrowed before the pressure meter; a CI check that the theme colour reaches the page and the payment window is missing (the pixel gate is local only); `getPageRow` is a sixth parallel query on a pool of three and could fold into `getCreator`. **Not verified:** migration 0051 on real Neon (it runs in the in-memory test database); the real page route against Neon and a real creator row (the browser tests have no database, so the real route takes the default document there; `getPageRow` has no test against a database); a stored custom page shown for a real creator (nothing can publish one yet; the parser and the choice are unit-tested); the console box with a real owner login; the live pages on a real phone and the owner page in a real browser; the iPhone (WebKit) test profile (not installed on this Mac, so only CI runs it); the pixel baseline is local to this Mac's Chrome (it is git-ignored; CI runs the text snapshots only). **Owner to do:** run `0051_page_builder.sql` in Neon, deploy, open `freetip.in/acepressure` and a normal creator's page on a phone and a laptop and say whether they look the same as before, tick "Custom tip page" for a test creator and look at its box in `/admin/creators/<id>`, and say what looks different if anything. **Next:** the phase 2 build plan (the editor with a live preview, Publish, the rank and text settings);

The Pressure meter is a block and an OBS overlay that follows whichever goal the creator picked (daily, weekly, monthly or never repeating), resetting on that goal's own schedule. Five phases: foundation (gate: screenshots match today's pages), editor, assets, meter, later (OBS overlays in the builder, copy a page as a template). Nothing is built, no migration yet.
