**DB:** run `db/schema-users.sql` once in the Neon SQL Editor.

Three animation correctness fixes (after the splash/title-screen feature):

- `SplashScreen.tsx`: removed dead `splash-done` class addition (CSS was already updated to use `page-scrolled`; the old class had no effect).
- `globals.css`: `.rise` and `.enter` animations are paused while `body` lacks `page-scrolled`, so they play from the start when the user first scrolls rather than silently completing during the invisible splash phase.
- `GlobalBackground.tsx`: checks for `page-scrolled` on mount so it sinks immediately under reduced-motion (where the SplashScreen adds the class in a layout effect before this component's regular effect runs).

Commit `f72dd37`–`f6175c4` added:

- A full-viewport `SplashScreen` that plays the hands animation once on page load, then fades out.
- A `GlobalBackground` with orbiting ₹ notes/coins and the glitch wordmark. After the splash it shows at full opacity as a title-screen moment; it sinks to `blur(28px) / opacity 0.22` on the user's first scroll/wheel/touchmove, while the page content simultaneously fades in (both 600 ms).
- `globals.css`: `.page-content` is opacity 0 until `body.page-scrolled` is set; animations inside it are paused during that phase.

Commit `186664c` added and pushed:

- A flat SVG handoff animation in the hero: one hand brings a lime ₹ note, the other receives it, and both retreat. Reduced motion holds a static handoff frame.
- A "Why Tipsite" section covering the competitor-derived choices: no fan account or email, anonymous tips, UPI + card, QR-first sharing and safer approved text-to-speech.
- A clearly labelled non-scannable sample QR illustration; live creator QR generation is not implemented.
- Sample-card labels for optional names, anonymous tipping, message limits and approval-before-play.
- Updated stream copy, FAQ, primary navigation, footer navigation, README, product notes and project guidance.

The landing page presents these as samples or planned product behavior. It does not claim that the backend already exists.

## Not implemented yet

- Razorpay checkout, payment verification or INR payouts.
- Creator tip pages, handles, dashboards or authentication.
- Live QR generation per creator.
- OBS connection or real text-to-speech playback.
- Message-limit enforcement, profanity/banned-word filtering and creator approval controls.
- Real accounts (sign-in method undecided) and launch emails to the waitlist.

## Decisions

- Tips by UPI or card, no fan account. Payments through Razorpay with INR payouts (planned, not integrated).
- OBS stream alerts and text-to-speech of the fan's message, so fans can add an optional name and message.
- Platform fee: 5% of every tip. Razorpay's charges sit on top of the 5% and are paid by the creator out of the payout; fans pay exactly the tip (owner, 2026-10-14). At Razorpay's standard rate (2% + 18% GST on that charge) a ₹100 tip leaves the creator ₹92.64.
- Product edges to carry into the future tip page: no fan email, anonymous tips, QR-first sharing, message limits, profanity/banned-word screening and creator approval before text-to-speech.
- Look: dark, flat, one lime accent.
- Audience: Indian live streamers first.
- Scope for now: landing page only.
- The page stays short, clean and to the point: only what is necessary, and something new rather than a copy of the reference sites (owner, 2026-10-13).

## Open questions

- Hands animation in the hero: the polished version is committed "for now" (owner, 2026-10-14). The owner said it is not what they imagined, so a further revision may follow.
- Payout timing, paid highlighted messages, product name and domain, sign-up and sign-in mechanism (email code, password or Google).
- Exact message length, moderation settings and the approval interface for text-to-speech (needed with the tip page).

- Legal drafts set these defaults for the owner and a lawyer to confirm: everyone (fans too) must be 18+; refund requests within 7 days, acknowledged in 24 hours with a ticket, decided in 5 working days, started within 2 working days, banks take 5 to 7 working days; grievances acknowledged in 24 hours and resolved in 15 days (content removal 24 to 72 hours); privacy requests done in 30 days; support replies within 1 working day; tip names and messages kept 90 days, waitlist entries at most 12 months after the waitlist closes, payment records up to 8 years; liability capped at 12 months of fees (creators) or the tip (fans); 15 days' notice of term changes, 30 days for a fee rise.
- Operator details still needed in `src/lib/legal.ts`: legal name, business type, registered address, court city, support email, phone, hours, Grievance Officer name, designation and email, GSTIN (if any), data region.

## Search engines and sharing

- `robots.txt` blocks search engines until `NEXT_PUBLIC_SITE_URL` is set to the real domain, so the placeholder name and the vercel.app address stay out of search results. Set it in Vercel once the domain is chosen. Link-preview fetchers (WhatsApp/Meta, X, LinkedIn, Slack, Telegram, Discord) are allowed in meanwhile, since Meta's skips the preview when robots.txt turns it away.
- Draft policy pages carry `noindex` and stay out of the sitemap while `LEGAL_DRAFT` is true.
- Link previews use `src/app/opengraph-image.tsx` (fonts in `src/assets/fonts`, OFL-licensed). Without `NEXT_PUBLIC_SITE_URL`, Vercel's production address is used for the image link.

## Waitlist setup (done 2026-10-16; kept for a rebuild)

1. Create a free Neon project (region: Singapore or Mumbai if offered, closest to India) and copy the **pooled** connection string.
2. In the Neon console, open the SQL Editor, paste `db/schema.sql` and run it. Running it again is safe.
3. In Vercel, Project settings > Environment Variables, add `DATABASE_URL` (the pooled string), `WAITLIST_SALT` and `CRON_SECRET` (each from `openssl rand -hex 32`), then redeploy. `vercel.json` schedules the daily clean-up once the project is on Vercel.
4. `dataRegion` in `src/lib/legal.ts` names the Neon region for the privacy policy (Singapore); keep it and `regions` in `vercel.json` in step if the database moves.
5. To see sign-ups: Neon SQL Editor, `select * from waitlist order by created_at desc;`.

How it behaves: emails are trimmed and lower-cased, one entry per address. A repeat sign-up gets the same "You're on the list" answer and leaves the first entry untouched, so the form never reveals who has joined. Handles are noted, not reserved. Each entry stores the privacy notice version (`WAITLIST_CONSENT_VERSION` in `src/lib/legal.ts`). At most 20 saved sign-ups per network per hour; only an HMAC of the IP is stored, kept for the hour the limit needs and deleted on every sign-up and by the daily job (the privacy policy says within 48 hours at the latest).

## Next

See "Next steps" in `docs/next-steps.md`; that is the single list.

## Working agreement

Finished, verified changes (lint, type check and build pass) are committed and pushed to `main` together with an update to this file.

## Verification

Every change runs `npm run lint`, `npx tsc --noEmit`, `npm run build` and `git diff --check`. Since 2026-10-15 there is also a Playwright suite, `npm run test:e2e` (see README); run it before committing changes to pages or forms.

## Implemented in the latest change (2026-10-21, second commit)

- **GlobalBackground title-screen + sink**: after the splash the background (glitch wordmark + money orbit) is now at full opacity with no blur — a brief title-screen moment. On the user's first scroll, wheel or touch-move it sinks to `blur(28px) / opacity 0.22` over 600 ms simultaneously with the page content fading in. Wordmark resized to `clamp(5rem, min(28vw, 45vh), 28rem)` so it scales to the viewport on any device and fits on landscape mobile (constrained by 45 vh).
- **Content gated on scroll**: `.page-content` starts at `opacity: 0` and transitions to `opacity: 1` when `page-scrolled` is added to body on first scroll. Reduced-motion users skip the title-screen: both `splash-done` and `page-scrolled` are added immediately so content appears without needing a scroll. Lint, typecheck, build and `git diff --check` passed.

## Implemented in the previous change (2026-10-21, first commit)

- **Fullscreen splash intro**: `SplashScreen` component plays the handshake animation fullscreen (fixed, z-100, bg-ground) on every page load. When the animation completes the overlay fades out over 700 ms, revealing the landing page. Reduced-motion users get an instant fade (motion-reduce:duration-0). HeroHands gained an `immediate` prop that skips ScrollTrigger and plays on mount.
- **Global animated background**: `GlobalBackground` component renders a fixed full-viewport layer (z-[-1], pointer-events-none) behind the entire landing page — the orbiting ₹ notes and coins plus the glitching "Tipsite" wordmark, blurred 30 px and dimmed to 17% opacity via inline CSS. Pauses on hidden tab via visibilitychange. Content above it reads clearly; the effect reads as ambient light from below.
- **Cleanup**: removed `HeroHands` from the hero section (it now lives only in the splash). Removed local `MoneyOrbit` from the Closer section (replaced by the global background). Removed the `handsDone` state from Hero; TipCard now starts ready immediately.

- 2026-11-03: Easebuzz called the owner and declined to onboard Freetip: they do not onboard social media platforms. No reason beyond that was recorded, and nothing was built on Easebuzz, so no code changes. Easebuzz is closed as an option. Still open for split settlement: Cashfree Easy Split (`docs/cashfree-email-draft.md`), Razorpay Route (`docs/razorpay-email-draft.md`), or creators collecting into their own accounts. Lesson for the next calls: say plainly that Freetip is a tip-collection page for creators with no feed, posts or hosted content, and ask the aggregator up front whether creator tipping or donations is an accepted category; do not relabel the business.
- 2026-11-03: deeper public-only research on how competitors pay creators (three independent reviewers; no logins, no backend calls); results are in the new last section of `docs/competitor-findings.md`. Short version: HyperChat is a pass-through with one Razorpay account and manual payouts (about 85% sure it collects centrally); supertips.store is probably the same but its payout side is not visible; supertip.live (a different product) says creators receive directly into their own connected account (second-hand); the old HyperChat price list was per-creator settings, not platform prices (corrected). Razorpay replied: Route needs domestic turnover above ₹40 lakh, RazorpayX above ₹1.5 crore. Cashfree has not replied. Nothing built, no migration. Owner to decide: launch small with a manual payout sheet (the pass-through risk in `docs/rules-check.md` stays), or pursue creator-owned accounts. Suggested emails: Razorpay's partner team about the Technology Partner (OAuth) programme, Cashfree about Embedded Payments as well as Easy Split, and PayU about split settlements.
- 2026-11-03: PayU is now the main route for split settlement. On a call PayU said it will do split settlement for Freetip (about 2% plus GST taken from creators, no creator KYC work on our side), asked for the owner's latest ITR and a Udyam certificate, and the owner finished both (Udyam registered as a sole proprietorship, services, non-trading) and completed PayU's KYC the same day. Owner did not enable international cards (more paperwork, about 3% cost, more chargebacks; still on the not-taken list). The one thing not yet in writing: the split agreement, the exact fee and who pays the GST, the settlement delay, who carries chargebacks and creator KYC liability, and test keys. Nothing is built yet and `src/lib/money.ts` is unchanged: the 2.75% processing charge was set against Razorpay's cost, so check it against PayU's real fee once it is written down. Cashfree sent only a generic marketing email (default 1.95% plus GST, T+2, Easy Split listed, no split fee or eligibility); kept as a price comparison only. Easebuzz declined earlier. No personal details (address, PAN, URN, bank) are recorded here on purpose.
- 2026-11-03: PayU's signed service agreement read (the PDF is kept outside the repo; it holds personal details). Pricing: a Platform Features Fee of 2% of gross value, daily, on every method, plus GST; no transaction fee on UPI, UPI QR, RuPay debit, standard credit and debit cards or netbanking; 1% on other, prepaid, corporate and international cards and on EMIs; 2% on other wallets and Pay by Rewards; optional priority settlement 0.08% to 0.30%. Real cost with 18% GST: 2.36% for UPI and standard cards (our 2.75% still keeps about 0.39%, same as the Razorpay case), 3.54% for other cards and 4.72% for other wallets (a loss on those). This assumes GST is charged on top, which the agreement only implies ("exclusive of GST"). `src/lib/money.ts` left unchanged on purpose. The agreement is a standard merchant agreement: it has no split settlement terms and settles to the owner's own bank account, so on its own it does not remove the pass-through risk in `docs/rules-check.md`. Still needed from PayU in writing: the split settlement addendum (how creators are paid, chargebacks, creator KYC liability, settlement delay, test keys). The owner is asking PayU. Nothing built, no migration.

- 2026-11-04: independent review of the PayU purchase (steps A and B together) before live keys: three independent reviewers (money and signatures; entitlement and dates; buyer-facing side), code read only, nothing run. No way to get a pass without paying was found; hash layouts, Verify, amounts, binding to creator and plan, exactly-once and test-versus-live separation were read as correct. **Nothing fixed yet; the owner picks.** Findings, ranked: (1) a paid creator can wait up to about 24 hours (the page only re-reads the database; the daily 18:05 UTC job is the only retry), and a failed first attempt followed by a paid second one whose notices are lost shows "did not go through" while money was taken (`plan-orders.ts` failure branch never asks PayU); fix: on page load, ask PayU about the creator's own pending or failed latest order. (2) No owner-only live test: `buyingOpen("live", false, true)` is false, so the first live payment needs `LIVE_SALES_OPEN = true`, opening sales to everyone at placeholder prices; suggested rule `live: liveSalesOpen || isOwner`. (3) Features stay on up to about 24 hours after a pass ends (the switch-off is the daily job; gates read `paid_features` only). (4) "End now" leaves the paid order, so the early seat, early re-buy price and finance sale stay; the audit line records the end time, not the removed end date. (5) A Starter term can be paid for and give nothing (cover checked only at order creation). (6) A later change to a plan's feature list does not reach running passes and can remove a feature early. (7) The owner's hand ticks and pass keys cannot be told apart (a ticked key already on from the pass is removed at the end; an unticked takedown is switched back on when that creator buys Creator). (8) Early seats can be over-allocated by one when a slow payment settles after its 2-hour hold. (9) No record of the creator's agreement (no agreed-at or terms version on the order). (10) Privacy text mentions only the mobile number; PayU also gets the name and email, and `legal.ts` does not name PayU or say how long orders are kept. (11) The receipt may print unreadably (dark theme, no print style; not tried in a browser), and `FT-<order id>` shows the site's order count. (12) The early-price rules are not public, and placeholder prices show to every creator while buying is closed. (13) No try/catch around the Buy call (a network error shows the dashboard error page); Cancel or Back leaves pending orders and 5 in an hour locks the creator out; a payment at a different price (a PayU offer) is invisible to the owner; the return route has no `maxDuration`; month arithmetic quirks (Jan 31 plus 1 month); a failed switch-off or lost reminder is silent. FAQ answers "What does it cost?" and "Is it live yet?" are out of date. Not yet tried against PayU: whether Verify can lag PayU's own success notice, a 200 in PayU's webhook log, and what PayU's `amt` means when a convenience fee is set.

Then the "fix soon" and smaller items, and the public early-price wording BEFORE `LIVE_SALES_OPEN` is set. The owner gives the final prices later. - 2026-11-04 (session summary, later): (1) Finance overview no longer fails when migration 0057 is not run (it falls back to counting every paid live plan order; pushed as ae3b992); the generic "could not read" message no longer blames migration 0023 for everything. The owner should still run 0055, 0056 and 0057 in Neon so plan figures are exact. (2) Cashfree Easy Split, read from Cashfree's public docs (nothing built): it is part of the Payment Gateway product, available on a Payment Gateway account; in production the owner contacts the account manager to enable it (sandbox can be self-enabled by API, which would allow a test build before approval); creators would be vendors (individual: PAN plus one ID, bank account or UPI ID; Cashfree ops verifies; statuses Document not shared, In Document Review, Completed, On Hold, Blocked); not documented: whether a tip can be split to an unverified vendor, any restricted business list, any eligibility rule. One vendor per tip (limit 5 per transaction). Split at order time or after payment (2-day window, default goes to the merchant); T+2 headline settlement, instant and on-demand options; deferred settlement can hold a vendor's share up to 45 days (account manager must enable it). Disputes are withheld from the MERCHANT balance while vendor settlements continue (we carry chargeback risk after a creator is paid; card disputes can come up to 120 days later, UPI about 45); refunds debit the vendor in proportion unless the owner chooses otherwise. (3) Cashfree prices from their site: standard gateway fee 1.95% plus 18% GST (UPI not separately listed, "as per applicable law"), no setup fee; Easy Split fee 0.20% (page also shows 0.25%) of order value on top; vendor payout and settlement charges quoted by the account manager. Festive offer: new merchants who signed up on or after 21 July 2026 pay zero platform fee on the first Rs 20 lakh of domestic Payment Gateway turnover until 31 March 2027 (one-time total per the pricing FAQ; one blog says monthly), GST still charged (about 0.35%), T+1 settlement included; excluded: Amex and Diners, corporate credit cards, EMI, prepaid, pay later, subscriptions, international, methods with a standard rate of 3% or more; can be withdrawn if credit cards are 60% or more of turnover (checked from Rs 1 lakh); once per PAN or bank account. NOT confirmed: that the offer covers the Easy Split fee. Rough cost per Rs 100 tip: about 0.6% to 0.65% during the offer, about 2.5% to 2.6% after, against the 3% processing charge. (4) Cashfree signup choices recorded above; the owner is waiting for KYC approval. Questions to put to the account manager in writing before building anything: does Cashfree accept a tipping platform for Easy Split; does the zero-fee offer cover the Easy Split fee; exact Easy Split and settlement charges; can a tip be split to a creator still being verified; can deferred settlement be enabled and for how long; who bears chargebacks after a creator is paid. - 2026-11-06 (paid plans bought through Razorpay, owner's request "map the paid purchase to the razorpay gateway"; Opus, high; **run migration 0060 BEFORE the deploy**): PayU rejected the site on 2026-11-04, so the Starter and Creator passes are now paid with the same Razorpay account and keys as tips. What was built: migration `0060_plan_orders_razorpay.sql` (`plan_orders.provider` 'payu' or 'razorpay', default 'payu' so old orders stay PayU; `provider_order_ref` for the Razorpay order id, unique; the Razorpay `pay_` id goes in the old `payu_ref` column). `src/lib/razorpay-plan.ts` (pure: config from the existing env, mode from the `rzp_live_` prefix, the popup signature check, order creation with `notes.kind = "plan"` and the amount checked on the way back, reading a payment and an order's payments, captured INR only is paid, refunded or another currency is a mismatch). `src/lib/razorpay-checkout.ts` (the checkout.js loader, moved out of `TipForm.tsx`). `src/lib/plan-orders.ts`: `createPlanOrder` takes a provider, `attachProviderOrder`, `failUnstartedOrder`, `settleRazorpayOrder` (mode, already paid, failure, mismatch flagged for a look, amount check, then the same `activate` as PayU; audit reason "Razorpay payment confirmed"), and the daily and page checks take either gateway and filter by provider (a Razorpay order Razorpay never heard of is closed after a day). `src/app/actions/plan-checkout.ts`: `startPlanCheckout` (no phone; returns what the popup needs) and `confirmPlanPayment` (signature, own order, then asks Razorpay). The Razorpay webhook settles a plan order before the tip code runs (`settleRazorpayPlan` returns null for anything that is not a plan order, so tips are unchanged; 503 if the keys are missing or Razorpay did not answer, so Razorpay retries). The daily job asks Razorpay, then PayU only if its keys are still set. `BuyPass.tsx` opens the popup, then reloads the page with the result; receipts and the admin name the gateway from the payment id. Privacy text no longer says a mobile number is typed on our form; TERMS_VERSION "6 November 2026, text 7" pinned. A Razorpay refund of a plan payment finds no tip and changes nothing (`postTipReversal` skips it); plan refunds stay by hand with End ... now. Checked: eslint clean, `tsc` clean, `npm run build` passes, 179 unit tests (plans, payouts, Razorpay, legal version, admin) pass, browser tests in Chrome pass (Buy form 12, tip page and fixtures 39). Read-only admin viewers do not see the box. Tests in `test/notify-email.spec.ts`. ## 2026-11-06 — AcePressure signature layer on the owner's tip page (not committed, not pushed; NO migration)
- The owner asked for their own page (`/acepressure`) to stand apart from every other creator's, using ideas from vengenceui.com. Owner-only pieces, all in `src/components/tip/Signature.tsx` (styles under "The owner's signature layer" in `src/app/globals.css`), written in CSS and a little script, no new package: animated crimson **rays** with a moving floor grid behind the page; the **name flips in** letter by letter in the heavy italic face (flips again on hover, slash wipes in under it); the **tagline cycles** its "/" parts out of a blur; the **form card spotlight** follows the mouse; the **Tip button** glows, has a shine sweep and a pulsing ring while it can be pressed; **"LOCKING IN"** kinetic letters while the payment opens; the **strip under the card is a marquee** (paused on hover); a **pixel ember trail** follows the mouse.
- Gated by `signature` in `TipPageData`, set from `isOwnerTipPage` in `tip-page-data.ts` (never from a page document, so no creator can turn it on and an edited custom page keeps it). Threaded through `TipForm` and `TipFormState` into `CreatorPanel` (new `signature` prop), `OwnerLinks`, `PageLayout`, `StripBlock`, `SendBlock`. Mouse-only pieces do nothing on touch; with reduced motion nothing moves. Screen readers get exactly what they got before: the moving marquee words are drawn by CSS `attr(data-text)`, and the recorded owner aria snapshot is unchanged.
- This work was first built by mistake in the old `Freetip-main` copy, then moved here edit by edit on top of the banner-tint changes above (those were left untouched). The "Razorpay errors" seen in `Freetip-main` exist only in that stale copy; nothing in the payment code here was changed or needed changing.
- **Verified here:** `tsc --noEmit` clean, `npm run lint` clean, `npm run build` passes (this includes the banner-tint changes). Browser specs against `next start` with `PAGE_FIXTURES=1` in the Mac's Chrome (temporary config, deleted after): `tip-page-blocks`, `page-assets-page`, `page-preview`, `page-editor`, `effects-page`, `page-decor`: 139 passed. Screenshots at 1440 px, 390 px, mouse hover and reduced motion looked right; other creators' pages draw none of it.

## 2026-11-06 — Banner effect colour fix: follows the selected banner, no lag (Sonnet, medium; not committed; NO migration)
- Owner reported: the effect over the banner did not change colour with the banner picked, and the preview was slow to update. Causes found in `use-banner-tint.ts` and `Decor.tsx`: (1) the colour was the biggest pixel bucket, so a dark banner gave a near-black effect that looked unchanged; (2) while a new picture loaded the hook returned null, so the effect flashed back to the page accent and then jumped; (3) the whole animated WebP had to download just to be sampled, and only after the tap; (4) a failed or dropped load was cached as "no colour" for the whole visit.
- Fixed in `src/components/tip/use-banner-tint.ts`: bucket choice favours colourful over big near-black or near-white patches; hue is kept and lightness is lifted on a dark banner (>= 55%) or deepened on a light one (<= 42%); the previous colour is kept while the next loads (no accent flash); failed loads are not cached; loads are shared (`inflight`); new `warmBannerTints(urls)`. `CreatorPanel.tsx` reads a ready-made picture's small `posterUrl` instead of the whole animation. `EffectsManager.tsx` warms every banner picture's colour on mount so a tap recolours at once. `Decor.tsx` always sets `--fx-color` (tint, else the accent); `globals.css` registers `@property --fx-color` and fades it over 0.5 s (none under reduced motion). The tip page, the Effects preview and the page-builder preview all share `CreatorPanel`, so all get the fix.
- Checked: `tsc --noEmit` and eslint on `src/components/tip` and `src/app/dashboard/effects` clean. NOT checked: in a browser. A local dev-server plus Playwright run was blocked by the safety check, so the colours were not looked at. Please open the Effects page and tap through the animated banner pictures and an uploaded banner.
- Known limits: a white effect on a white banner is still hard to see (now deepened, not eliminated). The Banner page preview (`BannerManager`) shows no effect layers at all, so it cannot show the tint; left as is.

## 7 November 2026: web-design-guidelines review of the AcePressure layer (Sonnet, medium; no code changed)
- Reviewed `src/components/tip/Signature.tsx` and the `.sig-*` block in `src/app/globals.css` (about 1326-1518) against the Vercel Web Interface Guidelines. Only these two files were read; `AmountBlock.tsx` / `SendBlock.tsx` call sites were not.
- Findings (none fixed yet): (1) `.sig-name` / `.sig-l-in` use transparent text fill with background-clip, so in forced-colors (Windows high contrast) the name can vanish for sighted users: add a `@media (forced-colors: active)` fallback. (2) `sig-ready` (css ~1503) animates box-shadow forever on the Tip button, and `sig-sheen` animates background-position per letter under four stacked drop-shadow filters: both repaint every frame; switch to transform/opacity on a pseudo-element. (3) `sig-morph` animates `letter-spacing` (layout) and blur. (4) `MorphText` (rotates every 2800 ms) and the marquee (32 s loop, pauses on hover only) run forever with no pause control, and the marquee does not pause for keyboard or touch. (5) Brand name heading has no `translate="no"` and no `text-wrap: balance`. (6) Stale comment at css ~1387 (still says "a dark band"). (7) `.sig-btn` has hover and active states but no `:focus-visible` style in this block: verify the Button base provides one. (8) Marquee text is 0.65rem uppercase muted: check legibility.

## 7 November 2026 (night, 7): professional redo after "looks like a small kid's 2D design" (Sonnet, medium; uncommitted)
- Owner kept only the profile frame; wanted sharper eyes as in the reference, and asked why the installed skills were not used. They had not been: this pass loaded `svg-animation`, `frontend-design`, `emil-design-eng` first. Lesson: for any visual work here, load the design skills before drawing.

- **Avatar decoration:** CSS conic-gradient crimson metal ring (registered `--dp-a` angle turns a highlight round it), red glow, a steel katana (brass guard, crimson lattice handle, glint) crossing behind (masked so only the ends show), embers lifting off the ring.
- **Nameplate:** carbon-weave crimson plate, top-edge light, slanted blade-cut right end with a thin bright slash, light sweep. **Banner:** smoky crimson scene (feTurbulence fog), light beams, glossy mask emblem with the same sharp eyes. **Profile effect:** three depths of glowing embers plus two blade-slash light streaks (once on load, then every ~9 s), soft red breath at the edges; no character. **Form card:** same language (lit chips, crimson gradient Pay button, glass fields), no cartoon outlines; animated buttons kept. The page accent is re-pointed to crimson inside the frame.
- Easing per the Emil skill: `cubic-bezier(0.23,1,0.32,1)`, press = scale .97, hover gated by `(hover: hover)`, only transform/opacity animate (plus the one angle property). Reduced motion: no animation.

## 7 November 2026 (night, 9): decoration placement, strict upload rules, saved library, admin sharing (Sonnet, high; uncommitted)
- **Plan:** `~/.claude/plans/kind-wandering-ripple.md` (approved). Phase 1 = profile picture + decoration only; phase 2 (not started) = plug banner, alert media, sounds, music into the same rules and library.
- **Run migration `db/migrations/0062_decor_library.sql` in Neon BEFORE deploying** (tables `decor_items`, `decor_grants`). Without it the Effects page shows an empty library, an upload says it could not save, and the admin Decorations page says it could not read; pages already wearing a picture keep it (the address also stays in `users.tip_decor`).
- **Rules, one table:** `src/lib/upload-rules.ts` (`UPLOAD_RULES`, `describeRule`, `checkPicture`, `hasAlpha`). Decoration: PNG/GIF/WebP (animated ok), no JPG/video, 3 MB, square (0.9-1.11), 512-4096 px, must be see-through. Profile picture: PNG/JPG/GIF/WebP, 3 MB, 256-4096 px, roughly square (0.8-1.25). Same checks in the browser (whole file read) and on the server (`saveDecorUpload`). `src/components/UploadRules.tsx` draws the list from the table.
- **Placement:** `DecorPlacement {anchor (ring + 8 spots), size, dx, dy}` in `page-doc/decor.ts` (`parsePlacement`, `placementBox`); old `frameScale` pages read back as the ring at the same size. Drawn in `Decor.tsx` (`frameStyle`), editor `dashboard/effects/DecorLibrary.tsx` (`PlacementEditor`, `LibraryGrid`).
- **Flow:** upload -> saved to the creator's list (`createDecorItem`) -> picked as a draft with live preview -> "Save to my tip page" (`wearDecorItem`). "Take it off my page" keeps the item; "Delete this picture" removes it for good (and for pages it was shared with). A worn picture that names an item is dropped on read when the item is removed or the grant withdrawn (`resolveWornItems` in `readTipDecor`).
- **Admin:** `/admin/decorations` (nav "Decorations"): preview, owner, look, who has it; Share (type handles), Withdraw, Take down; all write `audit_events` (`decor.granted/revoked/taken_down`, entity `decor_item`). A box on each creator's admin page lists their uploads and what is shared with them. A grantee still needs "Photo frame and banner effects" on; the share answer names creators who lack it (nothing is switched on silently).
- **Decision (owner):** a shared picture does not bypass `tipDecor`.
- **Tests:** `test/upload-rules.spec.ts` (9), `test/decor-library.spec.ts` (7, PGlite), 4 new in `test/effects-page.spec.ts`, `page-decor.spec.ts` updated for the new stored shape. 494 specs pass across tip page, plans, admin, routes, responsive, pricing, deadpool (Mac Chrome via a temporary config, deleted; `next build` + `next start -p 3300` with PAGE_FIXTURES=1). tsc and eslint clean.
- **NOT checked:** a real upload to Blob, saving and sharing with a signed-in session and DB, the admin page in a browser (no admin fixture), Safari, real phone. Left: sounds text vs WAV looked fine (the browser converts to WAV); music type text omits aac/webm (minor).

## 2026-11-07, phase 2 started: banner on the shared rules (Sonnet, medium; uncommitted)
- `upload-rules.ts`: new kind `banner` (PNG/JPG/GIF/WebP, 6 MB, min 800 x 200, max 4096 longest side, ratio 1.5-4, no video). Rule fields changed from `minSide` to `minWidth`/`minHeight` plus `shapeText`; decoration texts now read "at most 4096 on the longest side".
- Banner browser check (`BannerManager.tsx`, now reads the whole file and uses `checkPicture`) and server check (`saveUploadedBanner`) share the table; the free text is replaced by `<UploadRules kind="banner">`. New server dimension check: an old-style banner outside the shape is now refused on upload (YouTube copy unaffected).
- Tests: `test/upload-rules.spec.ts` +2 (33 pass incl. mobile projects); tsc and eslint clean.
- Left for phase 2: alert media (`creator_media`, `media.ts` limits: 3 MB image/gif, 8 MB webm), page music, sounds (text says MP3/OGG/M4A, token route accepts WAV only), library/grant pattern for these, admin page kind filter. Banner has no library yet (single `users.tip_banner_url`). Banner UI not seen in a browser.

## 2026-11-07, phase 2 continued: findings on alert media, sounds, music (Sonnet, medium; uncommitted)
- Checked alert media (`media.ts`, MediaManager), sounds and music against their server limits. Alert media text (3 MB image/GIF, 8 MB WebM, 10 s, 4096 px) and sounds text (browser converts to WAV before upload) already match the code, so there was no mismatch to fix and I did not force them into `UPLOAD_RULES` (WebM and audio do not fit `checkPicture`).
- Fixed the one real mismatch: `MusicUpload.tsx` text, error and `accept` now list AAC and WebM audio like `MUSIC_TYPES`.
- tsc, eslint clean. Not done: library/grant pattern and admin kind filter for banner, alert media, sounds, music (big, needs a design choice: per-kind tables vs widening `decor_items`).

## 2026-11-07, phase 2: library decision (nothing built yet)
- Owner chose: widen `decor_items`/`decor_grants` (not new tables). Looked at what it takes; only banner fits cleanly (images, same limits). Plan for the next session, banner only:
  1. Migration 0063: widen `decor_items.kind` check to include 'banner', `size_bytes` max to 6291456.
  2. `decor-library.ts`: `DecorUploadKind` gets 'banner'; list/create/remove/grant already generic.
  3. Wear = set `users.tip_banner_url` to the item's blob URL (new action in `tip-banner.ts`); read-time check like `resolveWornItems` so a taken-down or revoked banner stops showing (banner URL is read raw in `creator.ts`/tip-page-data today).
  4. BannerManager: "My uploads" and "Shared with you" lists; admin Decorations page: kind label and filter.
- Not planned: alert media (already has /admin/media review and takedown), sounds and music (audio, single file, sharing between creators not wanted unless the owner asks); they would need nullable width/height.
- Cannot be verified locally: needs a DB session and Blob. Run migrations 0062 then 0063 before deploy.

## 2026-11-07, phase 2: banner library built (Sonnet, medium; uncommitted)
- Migration `0063_decor_library_banner.sql` (run after 0062, before deploy): `decor_items.kind` allows 'banner', size up to 6 MB.
- `decor-library.ts`: `LibraryKind`, `DecorItem<K>` (default avatar/frame, so the Effects page is unchanged), `listCreatorBanners`, `usableBanner`, `bannerStillUsable`, `isLibraryFile`; `listAllDecor` counts banner wearers by `tip_banner_url`.
- `tip-banner.ts`: an upload is also saved as a library item (if the table is missing it still works as before); new `wearBannerItem` and `deleteBannerItem`; `dropBlob` never erases a library file. `getTipBanner` (creator.ts) and the banner page hide a banner whose item is taken down or whose share is withdrawn.
- UI: "My uploads" and "Shared with you" lists in BannerManager (Use this banner, Delete). Admin Decorations: kind filter, "Banner" label; the Share note names creators without the "Banner on the tip page" feature.

- NOT checked: the banner page in a browser (no fixture), a real upload/wear/share with a signed-in session, Safari, phone. A grantee's old banner file is not erased when they switch away from a shared one (guarded by `isLibraryFile`).
- Not built (by decision): library for alert media, sounds, music.
